नई दिल्ली, 9 अप्रैल , 1991 
__ का . आ . 12 14: -~ यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन भूमि 
में उपयोग के अधिकार का अजन अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) 
की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार पेट्रोलियम और 
प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . आ . सं . 2918 तारीख 1-11 
89 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइनों को बिछाने के लिए 
अजित करने का अपना आशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 
__ और आगे , यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने 
के पश्चात इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

अब , अतः उक्त अधिनियम , की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए, केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा 
अजित किया जाता है । . 
___ और आगे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल और प्राकृतिक 
गैस आयोग में , सभी बाधाओं से मुक्त रूप में घोषणा के प्रकाशन की 
इस तारीख को निहित होगा । 


MINISTRY OF PETROLEUM & NATURAL GAS 

New Delhi , the 17th September, 1990 


S .O . 1213.-. - Whereas it appears to the Central Govern 
ment that it is necessary in the public interest that for the 
transport of petroleum from DHN to K .GGS in Gujarat 
State pipeline should be laid by the Oil & Natural Gas Com 
mission . 


And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying 
such pipeline, it is necessary to acquire that right of user 
in the land described in the schedule annexed hereto . 

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub -section ( 1 ) of the Section 3 of the Petroleum and Mine 
rals Pipelines ( Acquisition of Right of User in the land ) 
Act, 1962 ( 50 of 1962 ) , the Central Government hereby de 
clares its intention to acquire the right of user therein ; 


अनुसूची 


Provided that any person interested in the said land may, 
within 21 days from the date of this notification , object to 
the laying of the pipeline under the land to the Competent 
Authority, Oil & Natural Gas Commission, Construction & 
Maintenance Division, Makarpura Road ; Vadodara- 390009.

में उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का विनिश्चय किया है ।

प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में

भोजत किया जाता है ।

प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उन भूमियों में उपयोग

गंस पायोग में सभी बाधामों से मुक्त रुप में , घोषणा के प्रकाशन भी 
इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

का प्रा . 1222 - - - यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन भमि में 
उपयोग के अधिकार का मर्मन अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की 
धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम और 
प्राकृतिक नम मंत्रालय को अधिसुचना फा० आ० सं० 2953 तारीख 
25- 11-89 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची 
में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइमों को बिछाने में 
लिए प्रजित करने का अपना प्राशग घोषित कर दिया था । 
____ और यन: सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उपन अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उप 
धारा ( 1 ) के प्रधान सरकार को रिपोर्ट ने दी है । 
___ और प्रागे, यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उन रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पम्पास इस अधिसूचमा से संलग्न अनुसूचि में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमिवों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार प्रजित करने का विनिश्चय किया है : 

प्रम, प्रत उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 को उपारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रदम शमित का प्रयोग करते हुए फेन्द्रीय सरकार द्वारा घोषि करती 
है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न प्रमुमुचि में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त ममियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने में प्रयोजन के लिए एलद्वारा 
अजिस विमा जाता है । 


And further whereas the Central Government has, after 
considering the said report decided to acquire the right of 
user in the lands in the schedulo appended to this notifica 
tion ;

section ( 1 ) of the Section 6 of the salt Act, the Central

said lands specified in the schedule appended to this nutifica

का . पा . 1 - 27 : - - यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन भूमि 
में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन अधिनियम, 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) 
की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 7 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार पेट्रोलियल और 
प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . प्रा . सं . 783 तारीख 27- 2- 90 
द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्विष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइनों को मिछामे के लिए अर्जित 
करने का प्रपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा 
( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और आगे सतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात् इस अधूिचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

अब , अतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) धारा 
प्रवास शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्वारा घोषित करती 
हैं कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा 
मजित किया जाता है । 

और पागे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल और 
प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग में सभी बाधामों से मुक्त रूप में , घोषणा के प्रकाशन 
की इस तारीख को निहित होगा । 

अनुसूची 
मवागाम सीटीएफ से कोयती रीफाइनरी तक पाइप लाइन बिछाने के लिए ।

of India in the Ministry of Petroleum & Natural Gas S. O . 
No . 2634 dated 6 - 10 - 90 under sub -section ( 1 ) of Section 3 
of the Petroleum and Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of 
Right of User in Land ) Act, 1962 - ( 50 of 1962 ), the Central 
Government declared its intention to acquire the right of 
user in lands specilied in the schedulo appended to that noti 
fication for the purpose of laying pipeline . 


का . भा . 1229 ~ - यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन भूमि में 
उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्मन अधिनियम 1982 ( 1962 का 50 ) की 
धारा 3 की उपधारा के प्रधान भारत सरकार केपट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक 
गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . प्रा .सं . 3152 तारीख 24- 11- 90 द्वारा 
केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमिषों 
में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए प्रजित करने का 
प्रपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

पौर यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त प्रधिनियम की धारा 8 की 
उघारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार का रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और आगे, यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपार्ट पर विचार करने में 
पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूचि में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग का 
अधिकार अजित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 
___ अय, प्रतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत 
शक्ति का प्रयोग कर हुए केन्द्रीय मरकार एतदद्वारा घोषित करती हैं कि 
इम मधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूचि में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा प्रजित 
किया जाता है । 

और आगे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल और प्राकृतिक 
गैस भायोग में सभी बाधामों से मुक्त रूप में घोषणा के प्रकाशम की 
इस तारीख को निहित होगा । 


And whereas the Compctent Authority has under sub 
section ( 1 ) of the Section 6 of the said Act, submitted report 
to the Government ; 


And further whereas the Central Government has, after 
considering the said report decided to acquire the right of 
user in the lands in the schedulc appended to this notifica 
tion ; 


Now therefore, in exercise of the power conferred by sub 
Section ( 1 ) of the Section 6 of the said Act , the Central 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the 
caid lands specified in the scheduly appended to this notifica 
tion hereby acquired for laying the pipeline ; 

And further in excrcise of power conforred by sub -section 
( 4 ) of the section, the Central Government directs that the 
right of user in the said lands shall instead of vesting in Cen 
tral Government vests on this date of the publication of this 
declaration in the Oil & Natural Gas Commission free from 
encumbrances.

का भा . 1230 ---यतः पेट्रोलियम पीर खनिज पाईलाईन भूमि में 
उपयोग के अधिकार का पर्जन अधिनियम 1962 ( 1982 का 50 ) की 
धारा 3 की उपधारा के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक 
गैस मंत्रालय क प्रधूिचना का. प्रा. सं. 3061 तारीख 17- 11- 00 द्वारा 
केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचमा से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियो 
में प्रयोग के अधिकार को पाईपलाईन को बिछाने के लिए अजित करने 
का प्रपना माशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

भीर यत: सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त प्रधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

पीर आगे, यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार मे उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूचि में विनिविष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजित करने का निश्चय किया है । 

अब, प्रतः उमत अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न प्रमुमूधि में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियो म 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एनद्वारा 
मजित कि जाता है । 

पौर मागे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्री । सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल और प्राकृतिक 
गैस प्रयाग में सभी बाधामों से मुक्त रूप में , घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस

जाता है ।

50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रो 
लियम और प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . आ . सं . 2957 
सारीख 6-11- 89 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना में संलग्न 
अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइनों 
को बिछाने के लिए अजित करने का अपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया 
था । 
__ और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट के वी है । 

और मागे , यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विवार करने 
के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संजात अनु सूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में 
उपयोग का भधिकार अजित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

अप यत : उमस अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त 
पाक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा घोषित करती है 
कि इस मधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उप 
योग का प्रतिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा 
मजिस किया जाता है । 
___ और मागे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त मूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल और प्राकृतिक 
गैस प्रायोग में , सभी बाधाओं से मुक्त रूप में घोषणा के प्रकाशन की 
इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

[ भाग II - वंय 3 ( ii ) ] 

भारत का राजपत : मई 4 , 1991 / बैशाख 14, 1913 
• - - 
पौर प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . आ . सं . 2925 तारीख 
1-11-89 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से मंलग्न अनुसूची 
में विनिर्षिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइनों को बिछाने 
के लिए अजित फरमे का अपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

और यसः सक्षम प्राधिकारी में उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

पौर भागे , यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अर्जित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

प्रम , प्रतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्दारा घोषित 
करती है कि इस प्रधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट उक्त भूमियों 
में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतवारा 
मजित किया जाता है । 

मौर मागे उस धारा को उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का 
प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त भमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाय तेल पीर

की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

का . आ . 1239: - - यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन भूमि

की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के पधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम 
पौर प्राकृतिक गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . मा . सं . 3146 तारीख 
24- 11- 89 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस प्रधिसूचमा से संलग्म अनुसूची 
में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग के अधिकार को पाछपलाइनों को विछामे 
के लिए अजित करने का अपना प्राशय पोषित कर दिया था । 

मोर यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

पौर भागे , यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात् इस प्रधिापना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट भूमियों में उपयोग 
का मधिकार अजित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 
- प्रय, प्रतः उम्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रवरा शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा घोषित करतो 
है कि इस मधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट उक्त भूमियों में

का . मा . 1243 - - यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन भूमि में 
उपयाग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की 
धारा 3 की उपधारा के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम और प्राकृतिक 
गैस मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना का . प्रा . सं . 2652 तारीख 6- 10- 90 द्वारा 
केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में भूमियो में उप 
योग के अधिकार को पाइपलाइनों को बिछाने के लिए प्रजित करने का 
अपना माणय घोषित कर दिया था । 


S . O . 1243. - Wherees by notification of the Government 
of India in the Ministry of Petroleuni and Natural Gas 
S . O . No. 2652 dated 6-10- 90 under sub-section (1 ) of 
Section 3 of the Petroleum and Minerals Pipclines ( Acqui . 
sition of Right of User in Land) Act , 1962 ( 50 of 1962 ), 
the Central Government declared its intention to acquire 
the right of user in landa specified in the schodule appended - 
to that notification for the purpose of laying pipeline . 


और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उम्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की 
उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार की रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 


And whereas the Competent Authority bus under sub 
section (1 ) of the Section 6 of the said Act, submitted 
report to the Government; 


पौर मागे यत. केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के 
पश्चात इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न प्रनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियो में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अर्जित करने का विनिश्चिय किया है । 


And further whereas the Central Goveroment has, after 
considering the said report decided to acquire the right of 
user in the lands in the subodulo appended to this 
notification 


प्रज , प्रत. उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रवत शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस अधिसूधमा में संलज अनुसूची में विनिविष्ट उक्त भूमियों में 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा 
प्रणित किया जाता है । 


Now therefore, in exercise of the power conferred by sub 
section (1) of the Section 6 of the said Act, the Central 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the 
said lands specified in the schedule appended to this notifi 
cation hereby acquired for laying the pipeline ; 


मोर प्रागे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) बारा प्रदत्त पाक्तियों का प्रयोग 
करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश पती है कि उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग 
का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित हाने की बजाय तेल भोर प्राकृतिक 
गैस प्रायोग में सभी बाधामों से मुक्त रूप में , घोषणा के प्रकासम की इस 
तारीख का निहित होगा ।

sition of Right of User in Land) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962). 
the Central Government declaicd its intention to acquire 
the right of user in lands specified in the schedule appended 
to that notification for the purpose of laying pipeline. 


अब, अत: उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा 
प्रवास शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एसद्वारा घोषित करती 
है कि इस अधिसूचना में सलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमिया में 
उपयोग का अधिकार पाइपलाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा 
जिन किया जाता है ।

section (1) of the Section 6 of the said Art, submitted 
report to the Government; 

And further whereas the Central Government has, after 
considering the said report dicided to acquire the right of 
user, in the lands in the schedule appended to this 
Tlotification ;

उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने की बजाए तेल और 
प्राकृतिक गैस प्रायोग मे सभी बाधाओं से मुक्त रूप में पोषणा के 
प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

section (1 ) of the Section 6 of the said Act, the Central 
Government hereby declares that the right of user in the 
said lands specified in the schedulo appended to this notin 
cation hereby acquired for laying the pipeline ;

The Central Government has referred the following indus 
trial dispute to this Tribunal for adjudication under section 
10 of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947. 
" Whether the action of the Management of Food Cor 

poralion of India in not granting the same wages , 
status and other benefits of service conditions as of 
the departmental workers to the fwod bandling wor 
kers cmployed in their direct payincat system depots 
(us per Annexure ) 1 fair and justihed ? If not, to 
wbal relief and service conditions, the workmen are 
entitled and the date from which they are so en 
titled ? "

and aie eniitled to be treated on par with deparținentalised 
Labour. The Union deny that they had any time given up 
their ultimate goal of total departmentalisation of Labour 
add are thereſole cstopped from pressing the same in this Re 
ference. On consideration of all material facts , the Union 
urges, the DPS fubour deserve to be brought on par with the 
dcparimcntalised labour. Both are doing substantially indu

their statement of claim . The Union claims that retroupective 
effect may be given to the Award from the ycar the workmun

Initially Shri Justice M . S . Jamdar was appointed the Presi 
ding Officer of the Tribunal under section 7B of the Act. 
Atter his retirement, a fresh notification was issued by the 
Central Government on 4 - 1 - 1990 appointing me in his place .

2 . The Food Corporation of India (hereafter the Corpo . 
l ation ) was constituted under Food Corporation Act, 1964. 
Before thç advent of this Act, inc activities of the Corpura 
tion were being managed departmentally by the Ministry of 
Food at the Centrc. The Corporation started their activities 
101 196 ) . For administrative convenience , they have divided 
the country into tive Zones (North , South , East, West and 
Northcast ) These Zones ale lurther divided into 21 regions. 
There arç about 2000 depots dispersed throughout the length 
and breadth of the Country . Some of these depois ure owned 
by the Corporation , while others are taken on hire . Initially 
the Corporation had adopted the system of appujating Con 
tractors for Food handling, which includes loading, unloading, 
stacking and other ullied laborious jobs. In 1970 and 1971 
the Corporation departmentalised about lour thousand Work 
men in West Bengal and Bibar regions. According to the 
Food Corporation of India Workers Union (hereafter the 
Union ) who are uspousing the cause of Workmen in the 
present reference , a settlement was reached between them 
and the Corporation on 23rd May 1973 , under which the 
latter agreed to departmentalise 17 sell -owned depots spread 
Over Bihar , U . P ., Orissa , Assam and Delhi regions. The names 
of thcsc places are given in the agreement Ex. W - 1 . This 
departmentalisation was to come ott by 15 -6 - 1973 . As regards 
other self -owned and hired depots in the aforesaid regions , 
the Corporation undertook to abolish the contract labour 
system and introduce Direct Payment System ( for short 
DPS ) , whereunder the Handling Labour were to be treated 
as direct employecs of the Corporation. This was to be done 
by 15 -6 - 1973 . Further the self -owned depots were to be for 
mally departmentalised by November 1973. The grievance 
of the union is that the Corporation went back on its com 
mitment, with the result that by year 1984 , DPS was prc 
vailing 10 only 41 depots and complete departmentalisation 
in 39 . It appears that there was one more system known as 
Labour Mate System under which the so - called Mate (ag 
distinguished from the contractor ) of a gang of 14 handling 
muzdoors used to reccive payment of the entire gang s wages 
from the Corporation and distribute them to the members 
who were his co - workers ( and not servants as in Contract 
Labour System ) . Ou an industrial dispute raised by the pre 
prosent union in respect of handling mazdoors of 14 depots 
having this Labour Matc System , the National Tribunal 
Bombuy presided over by Dr. Justice Tulpule held in NPB 
Reference 2 of 1985 that these mazdoors were direct em 
ployees of the Corporation and as such entitled to boulis un 
par with departmentaliscd Labour . After the award was 
given in that reference on 24th December, 1985, the Union 
and the Corporation cached a settlement on 3rd July , 1986 
hereby the Corporation abolished 1.abour Mate Systein in thesc 
14 Jeputs and introduced DPP . The final position now is that 
DPS prevails in 35 depots (listed in the annexure to the order 
of reference ) and full departmentalisation in 39 . It is the 
hi ndling mazdoors in the aforesaid 55 depots that we are 
concerned with in the present reference . 


5 , Un mcrits, the Corporation denies that the UPS handling 
Mazdoors are their direct cmployees and that they are en 
titled to be treated on par with deparimentalised Lawuul . 
According to them the duties of the two categories are Due 
comparable . The Corporation lanıcnts that their expulce 
with the experiment of departmentalising jy w Ostu 10.. 
been disappointing and the present move of the union s por 
justitied un mcrits and will be against public inter si ihuy 
point out that the cost of departmentaliscd Labour is as high 
is 5 times thut of DPS Labour and that thc Corporation will 
have to bear an additional financial burden to the time of 
15 . 5 crores of rupees per year , if the Union s demand 18 ac 
ceded to und that this will ultimutely he passed on to the ord . 
nary consumer. They have dealt with a numbor of minor As 
pects, including dillercal Labour systems prevailing in duerent 
depots . I shall refer to those aspects us , and when they emerge 
ini due course . Suffice it to say at present that the Corporatiou 
urc denying the claim in toto on merits too . 


6 . Both sides have fled further pleadings in the shape or 
rejoinder and sur -rejoinder, but these do not go beyond re 
iterating their respective stands adumbrated above , 


7. I shall naturally first deal with the preliminary objec 
tions raised by Shri Arora for the Corporation . By its noti 
ficat on dated 29 -6 - 1989 ( Ex. M - 1 ) the Central Government, 
after consuling the Central Board , has admittedly prohib . teu 
for good lle employment of contract lubour for jobs dono 
ly the handling mazdoors in the 55 depots under consideration , 
Indeed , this change had already been effected by the parties 
by agreement. The Government has done rutins heyond 
puiting its seal of sermanency on this arrangement. The sub 
ject matter of this reference is entirely different from that of 
the notification in its field of operation . I fail to appreciate 
the submission of Shri Arora as to how the notification forc 
closes the question hefore me, which is whether the DPS 
labour is entitled to be treated on par with the departmenalised 
lahour, 90 far as their wage structure is concerned . It is also 
difficult to appreciate the alternative limb of the Corpora 
tion s submission thru the Government could not have legally 
issued the notification during the pendency of this reference .

35, I shall now take up other objections deposcd to by 
Ganguli, which I have listed in para 30 gupra . It will not 
be realistic to dismiss Gangull s apprehensions out right 
regarding these maladies low output labour remaining idle , 
growth of indiscirline, monopolistic attitude on their part 
to disrupt thc public distribution system in exigencies / natural 
calamities. But then if we take a dispassionate view of the 
entire sconarlo , I cannot help feeling that the workmen alone 
are not to blame. I was distressed to read the incidents of 
vintenis indulari in by the workmen , as descrin . I in charter 
VI of the Asnani Committee Report Fx. M - 8 , and their 
consequential finiling No . 24 in Chapter VIT that in Northern

ellcative , rerraduce some other findings of this Committee

36 . There are also some more aspects which may deserve 
serious consideration by the Corporation is expected to lic 
an ideal employer in the State sector. Even the general prio 
ciples of sound business management ought to recognise the 
due importance of manual labour . Particularly in the field 
of handling essential commodities like food , sugar etc . AS 
the evidence ( including Asnani report ) goes , a bag of food 
crains weighs about a quintal. The standard daily outturn 
by a gang of 14 ( including 12 handling labour ) is such 800 
hags . It comes to about 67 bags per Labour . This Ilus 
trates the intensely laborious nature of the job . In their 
unique task of handling food grains and other essential com 
modities in a big country like India with a population near 
ing 85 crores. the importancc for the Corporation to have a 
contented willing work force ut their command can hardly 
be exaggerated . Our countryman have yet to realise in full 
nieasure that however efficient and imaginativc the head may 
he , it will virtually fail, unless it can manage to carn coope 
ration of willing hands . And neither the head nor the hands 
can give their best unless the body has a healthy sound heart 
located at the correet place that is, a little to the Left of 
the Centre . Ag an humble student of our Construction , I 
find this natural truth unambiguously enunciated in its 
Preamble and Parts II and IV ( directly material articles 
boing, Article 14 and 38 to 43- A ) .

37 . The Asnani Committee during their study tous did 
come across examples where the dopartmentalised Labour 
are giving an excellent account of themselves. Please see 
pages 35 of the Report, where the working of two dopart 
mentalised depots in Bombay - Sewrto depot and General 
Motors Bombay - - is discussed . The committee was pleased 
to note that the functioning was entirely satisfactory and 
both the Management and the Workmen were happy. A 
workman was actually earning Rs. 100 per day, as against 
the guranteed minimum of Rs. 63. This was the rcault of 
anplication of principles of sound business menacement, and 
the Committee expressed ull thc praise for the Management. 
The Committee found that the Contract Labour System was 
also not working satisfactorily . This provoked or tempted 
the workmen in their Unions at times to take to high 
handed tactics . For example, at one place - Shrinagar 
where the contract labour system prevails , the wage paid to 
a labour wag 1 falsa - to repeat one pais for loading or 
unloading one bag weighing about a quintal (page 58 of the 
report ) . I quite understand that this is an extreme case of 
inhuman exploitation and does not reflect the average posi 
tion . I am not treating it even for a moment as a common 
example. But such examples do help in keeping a discrcet 
ohserver alert and alive to the mandates of the Constitution .

the condition prescribed by section 10 ( 2 ) of the Act stand 
fulfilled . It has also evolved a formula to work out and 
decide as to when these conditions should be deemed to have 
hrer complicd with . One of the factors is that the labour 
should not remain idle over long period . T am mentionin ! 
this in the context of Gangrill s cvidence that one of the evil

linsurmountable . The foremost remedy is better Organign

instoncc. t my cite the case of J J. P . Deoot. Calcutta

Shri S . M , Izharul has been cha19c -shceted and placed 

under suspension for certain alleged misconduct.

heca considered by the DPC ." 
Lx. M / 7 is performance report which shows that he was

appears to be the adverse remark against him . When both 
thc docuincnts are read together it is apparent that his case 
was kept pending until disposal of the enquiry . There is 
uothing on 1ecord to show whether the workmen concerned 
Was punished or any adverse findings were given against tho 
workinan concerned in the departmental cnquiries . The pre 
gumption would , therefore , bo that nothing adverse could be 
found against him . That being so , he should have been 
promoted along with other workmen as prayed by him and 
there was no rtason why he should have been superseded , 
Thus the action of the managernent cannot be held to be 
justified so far the workman, Shri Izaharul Haque is concer 
ned . He is entitled to his due promotion in Munshi Cat. IV 
from 26 - 10 - 1979 as claimed by the workman and as Mupshi 
in monthly scale of pay with effect from 1 - 6 - 1982 with all 
the difference of wages and consequential reliefs.

13 . So far the case of the workman , Shri Aoup Singh , 
Luacler , is concerned , the management proved documents 
Ex, M / 1 to Ex . M / S and the unchallenged affidavit of Shri

G . C . Ramaswamy is inconsistent with the D . E . finding Ex . 
M / 4 which is photo copy of the finding of enquiry and has 
dealt with in cletail of the proceedings . It may be added 
that no other proceedings are on the record to substantiato 
that the workman had admitted the charge or that he was 
given an opportunity to prove his case . Thus this part of 
the testimony of Shri G . C . Ramaswamy has to be lejected 
which follows that the workman concerned was not given an 
opportunity to prove the genuineness of his absence during 
The relevant period. The enquiry certainly would stand vi 
tiated but in view of the fact that the workman is not taking 
interest in the case at all and not participating in the pro 
ceedings and further in view of the fact that the workman 
was absent during the relevant period without intimation to 
the management, it would be futile to reopen old pending 
CHSC of 1984 and to ask the management and the workmen 
to prove the misconduct of the workman before this Tribu 
nal. Eve n otherwise also the charges in the circunstances 
Hrç not such which justify the dismissal of the workman . 
If the workman were given Opportunity to prove that he was 
mentally upset and therefore he was absent from duty and 
could not inform the management about his absence, a more 
reasonable view would have been taken but in view of the 
circumstances of this case that the Union and the workman 
have been repeatedly noticed but they have not cared to join 
in the proceedings I find that so far the demand No. 2 is 
concerned the end of justice would be served if the work 
man is taken back in service without any back wages and 
consequential reliefs, but with continuity in service , My find 
ing , therefore on the above issues No. 1 to 4 are as under :

II . This is a peculiar case in which a narrow thread line 
Distinguishes this case from other cases inasmuch as per 
Ex. M / 3 dutcd 15 - 10 - 1981, Clauses 12 (1) (a ), (b ) and ( d ) the 
workinan had to disclose whether he was ever arrested , whe 
ther lie was ve prosecuted and whicther lic liad to furnisla 
bail , To this the workmun had saict no . The order Ex. W / 4 
howevcr tells of a dicerent story . A criminal case wearing 
No, 1337 / 80 was pending before a Juclicial Magistrate , First 
Clags, Sibora under Sec. 458 / 380 ctc . In this case the work 
man , Rim Prakash S / o Deoki Nandan , lus been arrayed 
als accused No . 17 . The proceedings dated 30 - 11-82 (Ex. W / 4 ) 
disclose that charge under Sec . 454 and 380 of 1. P . C . was 
not ordered to bo framed against him . Consequences are well 
known. The workman concerned must have been arrested 
during the year 1980 -81 because obviously a Challan was 
put up before a Court of Law in the year 1980 as the regis 
teration number discloses. Consequently , he must have fur 
nished buil also . Procecdings aro dated 30 - 11 -82 . Thus on 
15 - 10 -81 on which date the workman had filled in Form Ex . 
M / 3 not only a criminal case was pending against him , but 
he was arrested and bailed out, but he has given a false 
information stuțing that he was never arrested , he never fur 
nished buil nor way he prosecuted . Workman cannot avoid 
it by saying in cross - examination that he had simply signed 
Ex. M / 3 . The workman himself admitted earlier that he had 
no information of the criminal case against him but later 
on he had to admit that he had been uppearing before the 
Court along with his Counsel. Thus the basis of the appoint 
ment of the workman was on wrong informations . He must 
thank himself that his was a case of termination simpliciter 
He was not prosecuted for furnishing false information , not 
was he dismissed from service after holding due D . E . against 
him .

ray , from time to timc, by Notification declare 
that the Estates or Tenures of a proprietor or tenure 
holder, specified in the Notification , have passed to 
and become vasted in the State . Thus, it is een 
that once a notification is issued under the aforesaid 
Section , the entire estate , save and cxcept raiyati 
intcrest croated by the proprietor, vests in he State .

AWARD 
By Order No. L -20012 ( 3 ) 83 - D .III(A ), dated , the 
18th June, 1983 , the Central Government in the 
Ministry of Labour, had , in exercise of the powers 
conferred by clause ( d ) of sub - section ( 1 ) of section 
10 of the Industrial Disputes Act , 1947, referred the 
following dispute to Central Government Industrial 
Tribunal No. 3 , Dhanbad . Subsequently , the dispute 
has been transferred to this Tribunal vide Ministry 
of Labour s Order No . 9 - 11025 ( 7 ) 87- D . IV ( B ) dates 
31st December, 1987 |1st January, 1988 - 
“ Whether the demand of the workman of Bha 

gaband Colliery of Messrs Bharat Coking 
Coal Limited that Shri Rajendra Pathak 
should be promoted as Senior Overman 
from 30 - 6 - 78 is justified ? If so , to what 

relief is the said workman entitled ?" 
2 . The case of the concerned workman , as disclos 
ed in the written statement submitted on his behalf 
by the sponsoring union , Rashtriya Colliery Mazdoor 
Sangh , details apart, is as follows : 

The concerned workman is a permanent cmployee 
of Bhagaband Colliery of M s. B . C . C . Ltd . and he 
has been performing the job as required by a Senior 
Overman of the colliery . But he is not being paid the 
wages and emoluments of that job . Another emn 
loyee , Rajendra Pandey was also employed on the 
same date along with him in the post of Overman 
with effect from 1 - 1 - 73 by Office Order dated 16 - 1 - 73 . 
The management upgraded Rajendra Pandey to the 
sank of Senior Overman . While upgrading Rajendra 
Pandey the case of the concerned workman should 


3 . The case of the management of Bhagaband 
Colliery as disclosed in the written statement, bricfly 
stated , is as follows : 

The present reference is not maintainable either in 
law or on facts. The Overman is not a workinan 
within the meaning of the Industrial Disputes Act. 
Promotion is a function of the management and it is 
not the function of the Tribunal to consider the merits 
of various employees and then to decide whom to 
promote and whoin not to promote . Whether a parti 
cular employee should be promoted from one grade 
to higher grade depends not only on the length of 
service but also on his efficiency and other qualifica 
tions for the post to which he seeks to be promoted , 
and in the matter of promotion the intimate know 
Jedge of the higher authority, empowered to promote , 
has a greater value . In the absence of clear proof of 
malafide of discrimination on extraneous grounds ont 
the part of the management, it is wholly in appro 
priate for an outside authority to attempt to weigh 
the relevant merits of the individuals who might be 
holding the higher post and those who are aspiring 
for the same. The management has submitted that no 
retrospective effect can be given to an award prior 
to the date on which the specific demand which re 
sulted in the industrial dispute was made . Rajendra 
Pathak , the concerned workman along with others 
were promoted as Overman with effect from 1 - 1 -73. 
Promotion of mining personnel from Technical Grade 
B to Technical Grade A le from the post of an 
overman to a senior overman is based on merit - cum 
seniority and efficiency and suitahility of the sercon 
to the post to which he sceks to be promoted . The 
case of Rajendra Pathak was examined in 1978 but 
he could not be selected . There was only one post of 
Senior Overman and the most deserving candidate 
was selected . There is no merit in the demand of the 
workman and the reference is fit be to answered ac 
cordingly . 

4 . In rejoinder to the written statement of the 
management, the sponsoring union has asserted that

the overman is a workman within the meaning of In

ed workman to the post of Senior Overman is palpab 
ly inotivated and malafide . The case of the concerned

Pandey to the post of Senior Overman and hence the

5 . In rejoinder to the written statement of the spon 
soring union , the management has asserted that the 
care of both Rajendra Pandey and Rajendra Pathak 
was considered by the management and Rajendra 
Pandey was found to be of extraordinary merit and 
therefore he was promoted as Senior Overnan , 
Rajendra Pathak was not found suitable and so he 
could not be selected . Promotion is function of the 
management and it cannot be claimed as a matter of 
right. The concerned workman is not entitled to be 
promoted either from 30 - 6 - 78 or any other subse 
quent date and he is not entitled to any other relief . 

6 . The sponsoring union , in support of its demand 
has examined the concerned workman and laid in evi 
dence three items of documents marked Exts . W - 1 . 
W - 2 and W - 3 . Besides, the union has placed on re 
cord the conciliation file. 

On tne other had , the management has not exa 
mined any witness nor has it laid any documentary 
evidence . 

7. The concerned workman in his testimony has 
stated that he passed overmanship examination in 
1970 . He has produced photo copy of the overman s 
certificate under Coal Mines Regulation , 1957 dated 
4 - 5 - 1970 (Ext. W - 1). This certificate discloses that 
hc passed the examination held on 16 - 2 - 1970 and was 
granted overman s certificate on 4 - ? - 71) . He has pro 
ducel an order issucd by the management on 27 - 4 -72 
by the Group Officer to Bhalgora Group whereby 
he was promoted to officiate as overman at Kendwa 
dih colliery (Ext. W - 2 ). He has stated that Rajendra 
Pandey passed overmanship cxomination in 1972 al 
though he could not producc any paper in support 
of his testimony. Nevertheless tho fact remains that 
the management could not laid any contra evidence 
to dis -prove of this fact , Anyway , it is an admitted 
fact that both the concerned workman and Rajendra 
Pandey were promoted to the post of overman with 
cffect from 1- 1- 73 in Bhagaband Colliery. 

8 . Another assajlable fact is that Rajendra Pandey 
was promoted to the post of Senior Overman in the 
same colliery with effect from 30 -6 - 78 while the 
concemned workman has been promoted to the same 
post with effect from May, 1983 . 

The case of the management is that the promotion 
is function of the management and it is not function 
of the Tribunal to consider the relative merit of various 
employees and to decide whom to promote and whom 
not to promote . The management has further conten 
ded that the case of the concerned workman was ex 
amined in 1978 alongwith Rajendra Pandey , and since 
Rajendra Pandey was. found to be extraordinary 
merit , he was selected and promoted as Senior Over 


man while the concerned workman was not found 
suitable for the post and could not be selected . On 
the other hand , the case of the sponsoring union is 
that the management has made a palpable discrimina 
tion in promoting Rajendra Pandey over the head of 
the concerned workman and that the case of the con 
cerned workman was not examine i at all while 
Rajendra Pandey was promoted to the post of Senior 
Overman . 

9 . Indeed , the matter of promotion is a managerial 
function but nevertheless the decision of the manage 
ment in regard to this matter is not sacrosanct. When 
the management has resorted to discrimination , victi 
misation or unfair play in the matter of promotion 
certainly the Tribunal lias every right to look into 
the matter and to appraise the facts and circumstan 
ces. In the present case the union has specifically 
pleaded that the management has resorted to discrimi 
nation in promoting Rajendra Pandey to the post of 
Senior Overman while the case of the concerned work 
man was not considered at all. 

10 . Shri G . Prasad , learned Advocate for the 
management has contended that both the cases of 
Rajendra Pandey arid the concerned workman were 
considered way back in 1978 and since Rajendra 
Pandey was found to be extra -ordinary merit, he was 
selected for the post and the concerned workman was 
not since there was only one post of Senior Overman 
in the colliery . 

Shri S . Bose, authorised representative of the spon 
soring union has contended that the case of the con 
cerned workman was not considered at all and that 
Rajendra Pandcy was promoted over the head of the 
concerned workman without holding any D . P . C . 
which militates against the cadre Scheme. 

11. The concerned workman has emphatically stat 
ed in his testimony that Rajendra Pandey was promot 
ed to the post of Senior Overman on the basis of re 
commendation of the Officer and not on the basis of 
recommendation by D . P . C . He has further stated 
that Rajendra Pandey was promoted to the post of 
Senior Overman with effect from 30 - 6 - 78 while he 
was promoted to that post with effect from May, 
1983 and in the process he has lost seniority and suffe 
red monetary loss . In cross-examination he has speci 
fically denied that D . P . C . was held in 1978 and that 
he was not found suitable for the post of Senior Over 
man by D . P . C . The management could not produce 
any whit of evidence to show that D . P . C . was held 
in 1978 , Moreover , it is not the case of the manage 
ment that D . P . C . was held in 1978 . The management 
scems to have introduced the fact of D . P . C . having 
becn held in 1978 in order to whittle down the case 
of the concerned workman . In its comments before 
the A . L . C . (C ), Dhanbad, when the industrial dispute 
was raised , the management did not even plead that 
such D .PC . was held in 1978 . In its , comments to the 
AL . C . (C ), Dhanbad by letter dated 21- 12 - 82 the 
management has written as follows : 

.. As per Cadre Scheme of the concerned em 
ployees circulated vide No . BOCCL D ( P ) / 
CDY| 77127347- 89 dated 2527th May 977 
the promotion of Mining personnel from 
Tech , Grade B to . Tech , Gradę A i. e .

any period prior to the date on which a specific de

Indeed , this is a sound proposition of law but when

from which the demand shall be taken into considera 
tion , therc is no bar for the Tribunal to consider the 
specifically from that period . Anyway , in the present 
case the specific demand was made by the sponsoring 
union for promotion of the concerned workman which 
resulted in the present industrial dispute way back ou 
5 - 10 - 82 . The concerned workman is entitled to be 
promoted to the post of Senior Overman with effect 
from that date atleast. 

In the result, the demand of the sponsoring union 
sustains. 

14 . Accordingly, the following award is rendered 
the demand of the workman of Bhagaband Colliery 
of M s BCC Ltd . that Rajendra Pathak , the concern 
cd workman , should be promoted as Senior Overman 
with effect from 5 - 10 - 1982 is justified . The manage 
ment is directed to give him promotion from that 
date and to pay him other monetary benefit and back 
wages and to consider his séniority in service with 
effect from that date . 
In the circumstances of the case, I award no cost.

conferred by clause (d ) o tsub -section (1 ) and sub

Act, 1947 , referred the following dispute for adjudi. 
cation to this Tribunal : 


unblemished record of service . He had put în more

" Whether the action of the management of

the services of Shri Shymal Kumar Ghosal, 
Mining Matc w . e .f. 31- 12 -82 is justified ?

2 . The case of the management of Bhawanathpur 
Limc Stone Mines of Bokaro Steel Plant, Bhawanath 
pur as appearing from the written statement, details 
apart, is as follows : - - 

The present reference is not maintainable since the 
dispute has not been sponsored by any union nor is 
the same in - conformity with the provisions of Section 
2 ( s) of the Industrial Disputes Act, Shyamal Kumar 
Ghosal is not a workman within the meaning of Sec 
tion 2 ( s ) of the Industrial Disputes Act. Since he was 
never in the employment of the management, the 
question termination of his services w . e. f. 31- 12 -82 
does not arise . He was never einployed 
eïther in Kuteshwar Lime Stono Mine or in 
Bhawanathpur Lime Stone Mine for any work 
in any capacity whatsoever. The certificats 
issued to him by two officers of the manage 
ment in their capacity as First Class Mining Engineer 
were as per Mines Regulation and their acts can 
not bind the management. The contents of these two 
certificates are denied and the facts stated therein are 
not correct. The concerned person was never on the 
roll of the company and hence the question of viola 
tion of provisions of Standing Order or provisions of 
Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 do not arise . The cor 
tificate purported to have been issued by Shri N . 
Jankiraman ex -fadie shows that there was a gap ii 
thc purported employment from 28 - 8 -81 to 6 - 10 -81 
and this itself shows thot the certificate is not genuinc . 
Another reference case on the self -same fact is pend 
ing before Central Govt. Industdial Tribunal No. 2 , 
Dhanbad . In the circumstances the management has 
prayed that the concerned person be held to be not 
a workman within the meaning of Sec. 2 ( s ) of the 
Industrial Disputes Act and consequently he is not e11 
titled to any relief .

Koteshwar Lime Stone Mine in 1981. He has ad

. 7. Shri Sondhi as a Mines Manager and on behalf

be admitted as a candidate at any cxamination for a 
blaster s certificate unless the Board is satisfied that 
he has practical experience and training in a metal 
liferous mine for a period of not less than two years , 
of which not less than six months shall be experience 
and training in connection with shot firing. Had the 
concerned workman been engaged as an apprentice 
by the management, the certificate should have dis 
closed that position . I have already pointed out that 
the certificate does not disclose that the concerned 
workman was engaged in the maine as an unpaid ap 
prentice. That apart, the certificate itself shows that 
the concerned workman worked in the mine for more 
than two years and thereby qualified himself for ap 
pearing in blaster s examination . It has remained in 
explicable position as to why the concerned workman 
could not appear at Blaster s examination . In cross 
examination he has stated that since his practical ex 
perience was short by two months and 12 days he 
could not appear at the examination . But this position 
is obviously not correct because he completed more 
than two years experience and training in Kuteshwar 
Lime Stone Mine admittedly a metalliferous mine. 
Then again , the management issued him another cer 
tificate which has been marked Ext. M - 2 . This was 
issued hy Shri N . Janakiraman , Gerieral Manager 
* M & Q ) of Bokaro Steel Plant of Steel Authority of 
India Lt. 01 23 -7 - 84 disclosing that the concerned 
workman worked as unpaid apprentice in Kutcshwar 
Line Stone Mine between the period 10 -5 -79 to 
27- 8 - 81 on the trade of blasting and in Bhawanathpur 
Lime Stone Mine from 7 - 10 - 81 to 31- 12 -32 on the 
trade of Mining Mate . The managernent has tried to 
whittled down this certificate by stating in the written 
statement that since there was gap in the employment 
from 28 - 8 -81 to 6 - 10 -81, the certificate is not gen 
uine , I cannot actually comprehend the reason for 
disclaiming the certificate as genuine by the managc 
ment, specifically in view of the fact that the certified 
was issued by no less a person than the General 
Manager (M & Q ) Bokaro Steel Plant of Steel Autho 
rity of India Limited . If there is any gap that may be 
due to inany reasons including a contribution from 
the management itself Anyway, thefact remains that 
it is the firm case of the management in its pleading 
that the concerned workman was never emploved in 
Kuteshwar Lime Stone Mine and Bhawanathpur Lime 
Stone Mine for any work in any capacity whatsoever. 


Now , by adducing evidence the management has tried 
to make a ramification by stating that the concerned 
workman was employed as an unpaid apprentice in 
both these mines , I have already pointed out that tie 
cert ficalo Ex, M - 1 does not disclose that the con 
cerncd workman was cmployed as unpaid employee 
in kuteshwar Lime Stone Mine. 

The concerned workman has emphatically stated 
in conformity with his pleading that he worked in 
Auteshwar Lime Stone Mine from 10 -5 - 79 to 27 - 8 -81 
continuously as a blaster and that during this period he 
rendered service to the management by working 8 
hours a day . He has further stated that thereafter he 
was transferred to Bhawanathpur Lime Stone Mino 
by the management and that he was stopped from 
duty by the management of Bhawanathpur Lime Stone 
Mile with effect from 31 - 12 -82 . He has further claim 
ed that he worked for the management cntinuousiy 
and thereby rendered service by putting his attendance 
for more than 240 days in a calendar year. In cross 
cxani nation the management has not confronted him 
with the proposition that he was engaged in the mine 
as an unpaid apprenice , All that he has stated that 
his order of transfer from Kutesliwar Lime Stone 
Mine to Bhawanathpur Lime Stone Mine is lying with 
Sri B . Ram , Mines Manager , Bhawanathpur Lime 
Stone Mine and that he has admitted that he was not 
issued any pay slip and tride to explain the fact by 
stat ng that Sondhi Saheb told him that pay slip will 
he issued to him later. 

8 . The pleading of the parties and evidence on re 
cord boil down to the following facts - 

(i) the pleading of the management is that the 

concerned workman was never employed 
cither in Kuteshwar Lime Stone Mine or 
Bhawanathpur Lime Stone Mine for any 

work in any capacity, 
(ii) at the time of hearing the management has 

introduced certain Tamification in its casc 
by stating that the concerned workman was 

engaged as an unpaid apprentice, 
( iii ) the consistant case of the concerned work 

man in the pleading and evidence that ho 
was engaged as a workman by thc manage 
ment of both Kuteshwar Lime Stone Mine 

and Bhawanathpur Lime Stone Mine and . 
(iv) the certificate granted by the Manager of 

the Mines ( M ) indicate that he worked in 
Kuteshwaar Lime Stone Mine and gained 
practical experience and performed his work 
satisfactorily for a period of more than two 
years from 10 - 5 -79 to 27 - 8 -81. 


9 . The plea of the management that the concerned 
workman was an unpaid apprentice does not bear 
the test scrutiny . 

Apprentice under section 2 (a ) of the Apprentices 
Act, 1961 means a person who is undergoing appren 
ticeship training in a designated trade in pursuance of 
a contract of apprenticeship . Thus, it is seen that a 
contract of apprenticeship is essential in order to en 
guge person as an apprentice under this Act. Sec 
tion 4 (1 ) of the said Act envisages that no person shall 
be engaged as an apprentice to undergo apprenticeship 
in a designated trade unless- (a ) such person or if he

as a minor, his guardian has entered into a contract 
of apprenticeship with the employcr , and (b ) the con

Apprenticeship Adviser. 

Sub -section (2 ) of the said section envisages that 
every contract of apprenticeship may contain sucli 
terms and conditions as may be agreed to by the 
parties to the contract : provided that no such term 
or condition shall be inconsistent with any provisioni 

to this Act or any rule made thereunder. Thus, it is 
obvious that in order to cngage a person as App: 111 
tice contract of apprenticeship is essential. 

There is nothing on record to indicate that the 
management entered into an agreement with the work 
man for apprentice for any particular period or 
periods. Shri L , K . Sondhi has stated that there was 
no agreement with the concerned workman for en 
garing him as unpaid apprentice. He tried to wriggle 
the management out of this unfortunate position by 
stating that the concerned workman approached the 
management to allow him as unpaid apprentice and 
the management simply allowed him to do so . He has 
admitted that the management has not filed any do 
cumant to show that the concerned workman approa 
ched the management to allow him to undergo train 
ing as unpaid apprentice . Even assuming for the sako 
of argiment that the concerned workman did so , the 
mogagement had no right to allow him to work as 
linpaid apprentice without a contract of apprentice 
shin entered into hy and between the management 
and the concerned workman , That being the posilion , 
I come to the conclusion that the plea of the manage 
ment that the concerned workman was eniployed as 
unpaid apprentice is nothing but a figment of imagi . 
nation .

threatened to strike on or after November 7 , 1969

Officer, The Labour Court held that Shri Sharma was

quently , it did not nut an end to the disnute pending 
before a Labour Court . A writ was filed by the

2. प्रार्थी प्रतिनिधि अस्थिम । विपक्षी को और में कोई हाजिर नहीं ।

तथा “ नो डिस्पुट याच दिया माना है । 


___ 3. इस समझोत का पक्षकारों के प्रतिनिगि को पकट गनाया गया । 
कापर प्रोजेक्ट मजदूर यूनियन के अध्यक्ष श्री गेंदालाल शर्मा तथा 
परीधा सामग परियोजना मिक संघ के मरेटरी इन्द्रराज वर्मा एवं 
श्री एम . एफ . वेग तथा प्रबंधकों की ओर स श्री मनोज कुमार शर्मा 
ने इरा समझसि के तश्यो का राही होना स्वीकार किया है । एस गगमति 
के अनुरूप अधिनिर्णय पारित करने में कोई विधिक अाचन नहीं है , 
अतः इस बियाद में दिनांक 11- 12- 90 के समझौते के अनुसार अधिनिर्णय 
पारित किया जाता है । यह रामझौता ग धिनिर्गर या भाग रामदा 
आयेगा । इस अधिनिर्णय को केन्द्र सरकार का वास्त प्रमामान अन्तर्गत

New Delhi, the 19th April . 1991 
S .O . 1291. - In exercise of the powers conferred by sub 
secion ( 3 ) of secion 1 of the Employees State Insurance Act, 
1948 ( 34 of 1948 ) , the Central Government hereby appoints 
the 1st May. 1991 as the date on which the provisions of 

Chapter IV ( except section 44 and 45 which have already 
been brought into force ) and Chapters V and VI (except slib 
section ( 1 ) of section 76 and 77 , 78 , 79 and 81 which have al 
ready been brought in to force ) of the said Act shall come 
into force in the following area in the State of Assam 
namely : - --- 


"Shri P. S. Babu, 
Regional Provident Fund Commissioner, 
Bombay."
